\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{P}_{N}g,\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}(e^{-i\beta F})(\mathbf{P}_{\gg N}v)\rangle_{L^{2}_{T,x}}=0.

Therefore, from Hölder’s inequality and (
2.8
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{N}[\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}(e^{-i\beta F})v]\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{N}[\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}(e^{-i\beta F})(\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}v)]\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}\lesssim\|\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}v\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}},

and hence by (
3.6
)

\displaystyle\bigg(\sum_{N\gg 1}N^{2s}\|\mathbf{P}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{+}[\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}(e^{-i\beta F})v]\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}^{2}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|J^{s}v\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{4}-}\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}}_{T}}.

We consider the contribution from
\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(e^{-i\beta F})
. We decompose

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(e^{-i\beta F})v]\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}\leq\sum_{N_{1},N_{2}\in 2^{\mathbb{N}}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}v]\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}.

When
N_{1}\gtrsim N_{2}
, by Bernstein’s inequality, Sobolev inequality, and (
3.3
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}v]\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}e^{-i\beta F}\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N_{1}^{-1}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}(|u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}-s}\|v\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N_{1}^{-\frac{3}{4}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}(|u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{4}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}-s}\|v\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N_{1}^{-\frac{3}{4}}N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}-s}T^{\frac{1}{4}}\|u\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}_{T}}.

Then, since
s<\frac{3}{4}
, we have

\displaystyle N^{s}N_{1}^{-\frac{3}{4}}N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}-s}\lesssim N_{1}^{s-\frac{3}{4}+(\frac{1}{2}-s)\lor 0}\lesssim N_{\max}^{0-},

which we can use to perform the dyadic summations.
When
N_{2}\gg N_{1}
, then
N_{2}\sim N
and we take
\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}(e^{i\beta F})
into
L^{\infty}_{T,x}
and perform the summation over
(N,N_{2})
as in (
3.23
), together with (
3.6
).
Then, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}(e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x}}\lesssim N_{1}^{-1+\frac{1}{2}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}(|u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim N_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{2},

which allows us to sum in
N_{1}
. Combining all the estimates above, we have

\displaystyle\hskip 1.42262pt\text{I}\hskip 1.42262pt\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{4}-}\big(1+\|u\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}\big)\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}_{T}}.

(3.27)

We move onto
I I
.
By frequency considerations, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{lo}}(e^{i\beta F}u)]\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{P}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{lo}}(e^{i\beta F}u)]\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{lo}}(e^{i\beta F}u)\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{lo}}(e^{i\beta F}u)\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x}}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}.

(3.28)

We note that by Bernstein’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{lo}}(e^{i\beta F}u)\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x}}\lesssim\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.29)

Therefore, with (
3.29
) used in (
3.28
), we have

\displaystyle(\text{I \hskip-7.96677pt I})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{N\gtrsim 1}N^{2s}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{N\gtrsim 1}N^{-2\varepsilon}N^{2(s-1+\varepsilon)}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(|u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\sup_{N\gtrsim 1}N^{2(s-1+\varepsilon)}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(|u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}^{2},

(3.30)

for
0<\varepsilon\ll 1-s
. It remains to bound the second factor in (
3.30
), for which we write
|u|^{2}=|\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}u|^{2}+|\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}u|^{2}+2\Re(\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}u\overline{\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}u})
.
By the boundedness of
\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}
and
\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}
on
L^{4}_{x}
, and that
s<1
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{N\gtrsim 1}N^{2(s-1+\varepsilon)}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(|\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}^{2}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}^{4}.

By Bernstein’s inequality with
1<q<4
given by
\frac{1}{q}=1+\frac{1}{4}-s-\varepsilon
and Sobolev embedding, we have

\displaystyle N^{s-1+\varepsilon}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(|\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(|\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{4}_{T}L^{q}_{x}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}u\|_{L^{8}_{T}L^{2q}_{x}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{4}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{2}.

Finally, by Sobolev embedding, we have

\displaystyle N^{s-1+\varepsilon}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}((\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}u)(\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}u)e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}((\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}u)(\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}u)e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{4}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{4}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{2}.

Combining these estimates, we see that

\displaystyle\text{I \hskip-7.96677pt I}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{4}}\|u\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}.

(3.31)

We move onto the term
I I I
. By a similar argument as used for
I
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[e^{-i\beta F}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}u)]\|_{L^{4}_{x}}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{P}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}u)]\|_{L^{4}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{N_{2}\lesssim N}\|\mathbf{P}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}(e^{i\beta F}u)]\|_{L^{4}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{N_{2}\lesssim N}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}(e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}u)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}.

(3.32)

We first control each factor in (
3.32
). Fix
\varepsilon>0
sufficiently small. By Bernstein’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}u)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{LO}}u)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+N_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u)]\|_{L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+N_{2}^{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u)]\|_{L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}_{x}}.

(3.33)

Note that in the second inequality we used that the Fourier multiplier associated to
\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,hi}}
is supported away from the zero frequency so that
\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}
is bounded from
L^{\infty}_{x}
to
L^{\infty}_{x}
uniformly in
N_{2}
.
Let
r=\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon}
. By duality, using that
\mathbf{P}_{+}u=u
, and integration by parts, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u)]\|_{L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}_{x}}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|g\|_{L^{r}_{x}=1}}\bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{i\beta F}\overline{\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}g}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}udx\bigg|

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|g\|_{L^{r}_{x}=1}}\bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}}(\partial_{x}e^{i\beta F})\partial_{x}^{-1}[\overline{\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}g}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u]dx\bigg|.

Fix
h\in L^{r}_{x}
of unit norm. Then, by Hölder’s inequality and Bernstein,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}(\partial_{x}e^{i\beta F})\partial_{x}^{-1}[\overline{\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}g}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u]dx

\displaystyle=\sum_{M\gtrsim N_{2}\vee K}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathbf{P}_{M}(\partial_{x}e^{i\beta F})\cdot\partial_{x}^{-1}[\overline{\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}g}\cdot\mathbf{P}_{K}u]dx

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{M\gtrsim N_{2}\vee K}M^{-1}\|\mathbf{P}_{M}(\partial_{x}e^{i\beta F})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}g\|_{L^{r}_{x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{K}u\|_{L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{M\gtrsim N_{2}}M^{-\frac{3}{4}}\|\mathbf{P}_{M}(|u|^{2}e^{i\beta F})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{M\gtrsim N_{2}}M^{-\frac{3}{4}}M^{\frac{1}{2}}\||u|^{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N_{2}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{3}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}u)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\lesssim\|u\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+N_{2}^{-\frac{1}{4}+\varepsilon}\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{3}.

(3.34)

We reiterate that (
3.34
) was derived under the Hardy space assumption
\mathbf{P}_{+}u=u
.
Now, we consider the first factor in (
3.32
). By Bernstein and Sobolev inequality,

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}(e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{4}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{-1}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{P}}_{N}(|u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\lesssim N^{-\frac{3}{4}}\||u|^{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim N^{-\frac{3}{4}}\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{2}.

(3.35)

Inserting (
3.34
) and (
3.35
) into (
3.32
), we find

\displaystyle N^{s}\|\mathbf{P}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[e^{-i\beta F}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}u)]\|_{L^{4}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{s-\frac{3}{4}+}\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{3}(1+\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{2}),

where we have a negative power of
N
provided that
s<\frac{3}{4}
. We finally obtain

\displaystyle\text{I \hskip-8.25128pt I \hskip-8.25128pt I}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{4}}\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{3}(1+\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{2}),

(3.36)

completing the estimate in (
3.24
).

We now move onto the estimate (
3.25
). Fix
M\in\mathbb{N}
sufficiently large and decompose
u=\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}+\mathbf{P}_{>M}u
.
To control
\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}u
, we use the Duhamel formulation of (
1.8
) and obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u_{0}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+T\|\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}(u\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2}))\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}.

(3.37)

As
\mathbf{P}_{+}u=u
, in the second term
u\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})
, all input functions are supported on frequencies
|\xi|\lesssim M
and thus by Hölder’s inequality and Sobolev embedding, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}(u\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2}))\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}\lesssim M^{1+s}\|\mathbf{P}_{\lesssim M}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{4}_{x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{\lesssim M}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{8}_{x}}^{2}\lesssim M^{s+\frac{3}{2}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{3}.

(3.38)

Combining (
3.37
) and (
3.38
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}\lesssim\|u_{0}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+TM^{s+\frac{3}{2}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{3}.

We move onto the high-frequency portion
\mathbf{P}_{>M}u
and use (
3.17
) with
w\equiv 0
, to estimate each part in
L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}
.
We begin with the corresponding term
I
in (
3.26
). First, by the fractional Leibniz rule, (
3.3
) and (
2.8
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{>M}J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}(e^{-i\beta F})v]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}\sim\|\mathbf{P}_{>M}D^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}(e^{-i\beta F})v]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|D^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}e^{-i\beta F}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x}}\|v\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}+\|e^{-i\beta F}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x}}\|D^{s}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+}_{T}}.

(3.39)

Now, by the triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{>M}

\displaystyle J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(e^{-i\beta F})v]\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{>M}J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}\mathbf{P}_{\ll M}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}v]\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{P}_{>M}J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}(e^{-i\beta F})v]\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

and we estimate each of these terms.
By the fractional Leibniz rule, Sobolev embedding, and (
2.8
), we have

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{>M}J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}\mathbf{P}_{\ll M}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}v]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}\mathbf{P}_{\ll M}(e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{4}_{x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{4}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}\mathbf{P}_{\ll M}(e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x}}\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{\frac{1}{4}-s}\|J^{s-1}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}\mathbf{P}_{\ll M}(|u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{4}_{x}}\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+}_{T}}+\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+}_{T}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{\frac{1}{4}-s}\|J^{s-\frac{3}{4}}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}\mathbf{P}_{\ll M}(|u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+}_{T}}+\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+}_{T}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{\frac{1}{4}-s}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}}_{x}}^{2}\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+}_{T}}+\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+}_{T}}.

(3.40)

For the second term, we argue similarly:

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{>M}J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}(e^{-i\beta F})v]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}(e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{4}_{x}}\|v\|_{X^{\frac{1}{4},\frac{1}{2}+}_{T}}+\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+}_{T}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{s-\frac{3}{4}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}}_{x}}^{2}\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+}_{T}}+\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+}_{T}}.

(3.41)

This completes the estimate for
I
.

By the signs and the
\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}
on the second term of
I I
(analogous to
I I
in (
3.26
)), we have

\displaystyle\text{I \hskip-7.96677pt I}:=\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{>M}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{lo}}(e^{i\beta F}u)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|D^{s}[\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{lo}}(e^{i\beta F}u)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}.

Thus, by the fractional Leibniz rule (Lemma
2.1
), Bernstein’s inequality, and Hölder’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\|D^{s}[\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{lo}}(e^{i\beta F}u)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|D^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}(e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{lo}}(e^{i\beta F}u)\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x}}+\|\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}e^{-i\beta F}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}\|D^{s}\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{lo}}(e^{i\beta F}u)\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|D^{s-1}\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}(|u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{2}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}.

(3.42)

Now we estimate the analog of
I I I
in (
3.26
), namely

\displaystyle\text{I \hskip-8.25128pt I \hskip-8.25128pt I}:=\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{>M}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}u)]\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.

(3.43)

By duality, we reduce to controlling

\displaystyle\sum_{N\gtrsim M}\sum_{N_{1}\gtrsim N\vee N_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{N}g}\cdot\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}(e^{-i\beta F})\cdot\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}u)dx,

where
g\in L^{2}_{x}
of unit norm.
We split the summation into two cases:
N_{1}\gg N_{2}
or
N_{1}\sim N_{2}
.
In the former case, by Cauchy-Schwarz and (
3.34
), we bound this contribution by

\displaystyle\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}(1+\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{2})\sum_{N\sim N_{1}\gtrsim M}N^{s}\|\mathbf{P}_{N}h\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}(e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}(1+\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{2})\sum_{N\sim N_{1}\gtrsim M}N^{s-1}\|\mathbf{P}_{N}h\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}(|u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}(1+\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{2})\sum_{N\sim N_{1}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N}h\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}(|u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}(1+\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{2})\|u\|_{L^{4}_{x}}^{2}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{3}(1+\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{2}),

where in the second inequality we used that
s<\frac{3}{4}
.
If instead we have
N_{1}\sim N_{2}
, the
N_{1}\gtrsim N
and so by a similar argument, we have

\displaystyle\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}(1+\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{2})\sum_{N}N^{s}\|\mathbf{P}_{N}h\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\sum_{N_{1}\sim N_{2}\gtrsim M}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}(e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}(1+\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{2})\sum_{N}N^{-\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{P}_{N}h\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\sum_{N_{1}\gtrsim M}N_{1}^{s-1+\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}(|u|^{2}e^{-i\beta F})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}(1+\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{2})\|h\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\bigg(\sum_{N_{1}\gtrsim M}N_{1}^{2(s-1+\varepsilon)}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u\|_{L^{4}_{x}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{3}(1+\|u\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{2}).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{>M}\mathbf{P}_{+}[\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}u)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{3}(1+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{2}).

(3.44)

Thus, we have shown that for
\theta=\min(s-\frac{1}{4},\frac{3}{4}-s,\frac{1}{4})>0
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\ell,s}_{x}}\lesssim\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}}+M^{-\theta}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{2}(1+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{3}+\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}}).

This completes the proof of (
3.25
).
∎

3.4.
INLS regularity properties: a decomposition

We now consider
u
to be a solution of INLS (
2.16
), for which we want to show that
u\in X^{s-\sigma,\frac{1}{2}}_{T}
for some
\sigma\in(0,1)
and
s\leq\frac{1}{2}
, akin to Lemma
3.6
for solutions to CCM (
1.8
) in the Hardy space. In Lemma
3.6
, the restriction to functions in the Hardy space avoids certain bad frequency interactions in the nonlinearity. However, for a solution
u
to INLS, the equation does not preserve the Hardy space, and we must exploit alternative structure to show an analogue of Lemma
3.6
.
In particular, we start by rewriting the nonlinearity in (
2.16
):

\displaystyle u\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{P}_{+}u)\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})+(\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{lo}}u)\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})+w\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2}),

(3.45)

where
w
is the gauged variable as in (
3.13
).
The first term is like in CCM case where the output frequency satisfies
|\xi|=|\xi_{1}|+|\xi_{3}-\xi_{2}|\geq|\xi_{3}-\xi_{2}|
. The second term essentially has
|\xi|\sim|\xi_{3}-\xi_{2}|
, like in the first term. The main issue for INLS (
2.16
), as compared to CCM (
1.8
), is the third term in (
3.45
) which needs further work.

We write the third term in (
3.45
) as follows

\displaystyle\begin{split}w\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})=&w\mathbb{\mathbf{P}}_{+,\text{LO}}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})+\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[w\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})]\\
&+\mathbf{P}_{\textup{LO}}[w\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})]+\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{HI}}[w\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})].\end{split}

The first term is harmless as it essentially contains no derivative. The third term can be controlled adequately at least for
s>\frac{1}{4}
.
However, to observe a stronger smoothing effect, the second and fourth terms need further decomposition due to dangerous Low-Low-High to High interactions.
We then write

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{\pm,\text{HI}}[w\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})]=\mathcal{W}^{\pm}_{1}(u,w)+\mathcal{W}^{\pm}_{2}(u,w)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}^{\pm}_{1}(u_{1},u_{2},w)

\displaystyle:=\sum_{N\gtrsim N_{1}}\mathbf{P}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\pm,\text{HI}}[(\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}w)\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}\partial_{x}(\overline{u_{1}}u_{2})],

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}^{\pm}_{2}(u_{1},u_{2},w)

\displaystyle:=\sum_{N\ll N_{1}}\mathbf{P}_{N}\mathbf{P}_{\pm,\text{HI}}[(\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}w)\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}\partial_{x}(\overline{u_{1}}u_{2})],

(3.46)

and where, with a slight abuse of notation, we defined
\mathcal{W}^{\pm}_{2}(u,w):=\mathcal{W}^{\pm}_{2}(u,u,w)
.
In the support of
\mathcal{W}^{\pm}_{1}
, by sign consideration, we at least have
|\xi|\gtrsim|\xi_{3}-\xi_{2}|
which provides control on the derivative.
However, in the support of
\mathcal{W}^{\pm}_{2}
, we have
|\xi|\ll|\xi_{3}-\xi_{2}|
meaning that no amount of smoothing from the norm can help to control the derivative below
H^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbb{R})
.

To summarise, we have the following decomposition of the main part of the nonlinearity:

\displaystyle u\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{P}_{+}u)\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})+(\mathbf{P}_{-,\textup{lo}}u)\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})+w\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{LO}}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})+w\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})

\displaystyle=:G_{1}(u,u,u)+G_{2}(u,u,w)+B(u,u,w),

(3.47)

where

\displaystyle\begin{split}G_{1}(u_{1},u_{2},u_{3})&:=(\mathbf{P}_{+}u_{1})\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(\overline{u_{2}}u_{3})+(\mathbf{P}_{-,\textup{lo}}u_{1})\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(\overline{u_{2}}u_{3}),\\
G_{2}(u_{1},u_{2},w)&:=w\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{LO}}\partial_{x}(\overline{u_{1}}u_{2})+\mathcal{W}^{+}_{1}(u_{1},u_{2},w)+\mathcal{W}^{-}_{1}(u_{1},u_{2},w),\\
B(u_{1},u_{2},w)&:=\mathcal{W}^{+}_{2}(u_{1},u_{2},w)+\mathcal{W}^{-}_{2}(u_{1},u_{2},w)+\mathbf{P}_{\textup{LO}}[w\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}\partial_{x}(\overline{u}_{1}\cdot u_{2})].\end{split}

(3.48)

We point out that the operators
G_{1},G_{2},
and
B
are privileged to the location of
w
, which appears linearly. Consequently, we only need to vary the
functions
(u_{1},u_{2},u_{3})
when obtaining difference estimates.

We start by estimating the “bad part”
B(u,u,w)
of the nonlinearity in (
3.47
).

Lemma 3.8
.

Fix
\delta>0
small,
s\geq s_{0}>0
such that
s+s_{0}>\frac{1}{2}+10\delta
, and

\displaystyle\sigma>\max(\tfrac{1}{2}-s_{0}+10\delta,s-s_{0}).

(3.49)

Then, for
B
defined in (
3.48
), we have

\displaystyle\|B(u_{1},u_{2},w)\|_{X^{s-\sigma,-\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}_{T}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}_{T}}\Big\{\|J^{s_{0}}u_{1}\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}\|J^{s_{0}}u_{2}\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|u_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\Big\}.

Proof.

We only prove the estimate when
u_{1}=u_{2}=u
as the general case clearly follows.
By (
3.48
) and the triangle inequality, we need to show

\displaystyle\max_{\mathfrak{s}\in\{\pm\}}\|\mathcal{W}_{2}^{\mathfrak{s}}(u,w)\|_{X^{s-\sigma,-\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}_{T}}+

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{\textup{LO}}[w\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})]\|_{X^{s-\sigma,-\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}_{T}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}_{T}}\Big[\|J^{s_{0}}u\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}^{2}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}^{2}\Big],

where
\mathcal{W}^{\pm}_{2}
are as in (
3.46
).
By dyadic decomposition and duality, it suffices to estimate

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3},N_{23}\\
N\ll N_{1}\sim N_{23}\end{subarray}}[\mathbf{1}_{\{N\gg 1\}}N^{s-\sigma}+\mathbf{1}_{\{N\lesssim 1\}}]\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\mathbf{P}_{N}g}\cdot\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}w\,\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}\partial_{x}\mathbf{P}_{N_{23}}(\overline{\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}u}\mathbf{P}_{N_{3}}u)dxdt

(3.50)

where
g\in X^{0,\frac{1}{2}-2\delta}_{T}
and
\|g\|_{X^{0,\frac{1}{2}-2\delta}_{T}}\leq 1

and we have noted that in the support of both
\mathcal{W}^{\pm}_{2}(u,w)
and
\mathbf{P}_{\textup{LO}}[w\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})]
, we have
N_{1}\sim N_{23}
.

If
N_{23}\lesssim 1
, it is enough to place all functions into
L^{4}_{T}W^{s_{0},4}_{x}
and use (
3.6
), which requires
\sigma>s-s_{0}
.
Thus, we assume that
N_{23}\gg 1
. We first consider the case when
N\gtrsim 1
.

\bullet

Case 1:
N_{2}\wedge N_{3}\gg 1
.

By Hölder’s inequality and (
3.8
), we bound (
3.50
) above by

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3},N_{23}\\
N\ll N_{1}\sim N_{23}\end{subarray}}N^{s-\sigma}N_{23}^{\frac{1}{2}+10\delta}\|\mathbf{P}_{N}g\|_{X^{0,\frac{1}{2}-2\delta}_{T}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}w\|_{X^{0,\frac{1}{2}}_{T}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}u\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{3}}u\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3},N_{23}\\
N\ll N_{1}\sim N_{23}\end{subarray}}\frac{N^{s-\sigma}N_{23}^{\frac{1}{2}+10\delta}}{N_{1}^{s}(N_{2}N_{3})^{s_{0}}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N}g\|_{X^{0,\frac{1}{2}-2\delta}_{T}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}w\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}}_{T}}\|J^{s_{0}}\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}u\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}\|J^{s_{0}}\mathbf{P}_{N_{3}}u\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}.

Now we consider the dyadic factor.
Since
N_{1}\sim N_{23}\lesssim N_{2}\lor N_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{N^{s-\sigma}N_{23}^{\frac{1}{2}+10\delta}}{N_{1}^{s}(N_{2}N_{3})^{s_{0}}}\lesssim\frac{N^{s-\sigma}}{N_{1}^{s+s_{0}-\frac{1}{2}-10\delta-}(N_{2}\lor N_{3})^{0+}}\lesssim N_{1}^{-s_{0}+\frac{1}{2}-\sigma+10\delta+}(N_{2}\lor N_{3})^{0-}

where we have used that
s+s_{0}>\frac{1}{2}+10\delta
. Note that we have a negative powers above provided that
s_{0}+\sigma>\frac{1}{2}+10\delta
, which forces the first condition in (
3.49
).

\bullet

Case 2:
N_{2}\wedge N_{3}\lesssim 1
.

In this case, we handle
g
and
w
as in Case 1, and we simply place the term with
\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}\land N_{3}}
into
L^{\infty}_{T,x}
and use Bernstein’s inequality, while the term with
\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}\vee N_{3}}
is placed in
L^{2}_{T,x}
. This yields the same numerology on
(s,s_{0},\sigma)
as above, since we did not use the extra weight
(N_{2}\land N_{3})^{-s_{0}}
before.

Lastly, if
N\lesssim 1
, which is the case for
\mathbf{P}_{\textup{LO}}[w\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})]
, arguing as above in Cases 1-2, we impose no condition on
\sigma
and only need
s+s_{0}>\frac{1}{2}+10\delta
.
This completes the proof.
∎

Let
\mathcal{I}
denote the Duhamel integral operator associated to (
2.16
):

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}[f](t)=\int_{0}^{t}S(t-t^{\prime})f(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

which is the solution to the inhomogenous equation:

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\partial_{t}\mathcal{I}[f]+i\partial_{x}^{2}\mathcal{I}[f]=f,\\
\mathcal{I}[f]|_{t=0}=0.\end{cases}

(3.51)

Next, we obtain the
X^{s,b}
-regularity of
u
after removing the contribution from the bad part
B
.

Lemma 3.9
.

Let
0<T\leq 1
,
\delta>0
sufficiently small, and
\frac{1}{4}<s_{0}\leq s<\frac{3}{4}
such that
s+s_{0}>\frac{1}{2}+10\delta
. Let
u
be an
H^{\infty}(\mathbb{R})
-solution to (
2.16
) on
[0,T]
and
w=\mathbf{P}_{-,\textup{hi}}u
.
Then, for
B
as in (
3.48
), there exists
\theta>0
such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq\eta\leq 1}\|u-\mathcal{I}[B(u,u,w)]\|_{X^{s-\eta,\eta}_{T}}\\
&\lesssim\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}+\|w\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}_{T}}\Big[\|J^{s_{0}}u\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}^{2}+T^{\theta}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}^{2}\Big]+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\mathcal{Q}_{h}\|_{\textup{op}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{3}\\
&\hphantom{X}+\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}\big(\|J^{s_{0}}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\big)\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+T^{\frac{1}{2}-}\|u\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\end{split}

(3.52)

where
\mathcal{Q}_{h}
is as in (
2.15
).
Moreover,
let
u_{j}
be
H^{\infty}(\mathbb{R})
-solutions to (
2.16
) on
[0,T]
with initial data
u_{j}(0)=u_{0,j}
,
j=1,2
, and let
U:=u_{1}-u_{2}
and
W:=w_{1}-w_{2}=\mathbf{P}_{-,\textup{hi}}U
.
Then, we have the difference estimate:

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq\eta\leq 1}\|(u_{1}-\mathcal{I}[B(u_{1},u_{1},w_{1})])-(u_{2}-\mathcal{I}[B(u_{2},u_{2},w_{2})])\|_{X^{s-\eta,\eta}_{T}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|U\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|W\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}_{T}}\max_{j=1,2}\Big[\|J^{s_{0}}u_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}^{2}+T^{\theta}\|u_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}^{2}\Big]

(3.53)

\displaystyle\hphantom{X}+\max_{j=1,2}(\|u_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}^{2}+\|J^{s_{0}}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u_{j}\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}^{2})\big[\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}U\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}+(1+\|\mathcal{Q}_{h}\|_{\textup{op}})\|U\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}\big]

\displaystyle\hphantom{X}+\max_{j=1,2}(\|u_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|u_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}+\|J^{s_{0}}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u_{j}\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u_{j}\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}})\big[\|J^{s_{0}}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}U\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}+\|U\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\big].

Proof.

We show only (
3.52
), as the difference estimate (
3.53
) follows by a similar argument.
We first argue as in
[
47
, Proposition 3.2]
, where it is shown that for a suitable extension
\widetilde{z}
of a space-time function
z
on
[0,T]
, it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq\eta\leq 1}\|\widetilde{z}\|_{X^{s-\eta,\eta}_{T}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}z+i\partial_{x}^{2}z\|_{L^{2}_{T}H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|z\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}.

Then, taking
z=u-\mathcal{I}[B(u,u,w)]
and using (
2.16
), (
3.51
), and (
3.47
), we have

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq\eta\leq 1}\|u-\mathcal{I}[B(u,u,w)]\|_{X^{s-\eta,\eta}_{T}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(\partial_{t}+i\partial_{x}^{2})(u-\mathcal{I}[B(u,u,w)])\|_{L^{2}_{T}H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|u-\mathcal{I}[B(u,u,w)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s-1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|G(u,u,w)\|_{L^{2}_{T}H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|u\mathcal{Q}_{h}(|u|^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{T}H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}+\|B(u,u,w)\|_{X^{s-1,-\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}_{T}},

(3.54)

where
\mathcal{Q}_{h}
is as in (
2.15
), and we used (
3.3
) and (
3.4
) for the last contribution.

For the fourth term in (
3.54
), we apply Lemma
3.8
with
\sigma=1
. This imposes the second condition in
s+s_{0}>\frac{1}{2}+10\delta
and
s\leq 1
.
Considering the second term in (
3.54
), using Sobolev’s inequality and Hölder’s inequality, for
s<\frac{3}{4}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|u\mathcal{Q}_{h}(|u|^{2})]\|_{L^{2}_{T}H^{s-1}_{x}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\langle\partial_{x}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{4}}\big[u\mathcal{Q}_{h}(|u|^{2})]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}&\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u\mathcal{Q}_{h}(|u|^{2})]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\\
&\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\mathcal{Q}_{h}\|_{\text{op}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{4}_{x}}^{3}\\
&\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\mathcal{Q}_{h}\|_{\text{op}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{3}.\end{split}

(3.55)

It remains to control the first term in (
3.54
), for which we apply the triangle inequality and estimate the terms coming from (
3.48
).
First, we have

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{P}_{+}u)\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{T}H^{s-1}_{x}}\lesssim\|J^{s_{0}}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}

as the contribution is restricted to the same frequency regions as in (
3.20
), so we can proceed as in the proof of Lemma
3.6
.
Note that this term imposes the spatial regularity
s-1
.

Now we consider the second term in
G_{1}
in (
3.48
) which we write as

\displaystyle(\mathbf{P}_{-,\textup{lo}}u)\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})=\partial_{x}[(\mathbf{P}_{-,\textup{lo}}u)\mathbf{P}_{+}(|u|^{2})]-(\partial_{x}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{lo}}u)\mathbf{P}_{+}(|u|^{2}).

Then, by Bernstein’s inequality,
\mathbf{P}_{-}
on
L^{p}
,
1<p<\infty
, and the fractional Leibniz rule,

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}[(\mathbf{P}_{-,\textup{lo}}u)\mathbf{P}_{+}(|u|^{2})]\|_{L^{2}_{T}H^{s-1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{x}[(\mathbf{P}_{-,\textup{lo}}u)\mathbf{P}_{+,\textup{LO}}(|u|^{2})]\|_{L^{2}_{T}H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|\partial_{x}[(\mathbf{P}_{-,\textup{lo}}u)\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}(|u|^{2})]\|_{L^{2}_{T}H^{s-1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}^{3}+\|D^{s}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{lo}}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}u\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{lo}}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x}}\|D^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}(|u|^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}^{3}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|J^{s_{0}}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}u\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}u\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}.

Similar arguments can be used to control the other term
-(\partial_{x}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{lo}}u)\mathbf{P}_{+}(|u|^{2})
.

Now we consider the first term in
G_{2}
in (
3.48
). Since
s\leq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\|w\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{LO}}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{T}H^{s-1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{LO}}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{\textup{LO}}\mathbf{P}_{+}\partial_{x}(|u|^{2})\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}\||u|^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{1+}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}^{2},

where we used Bernstein’s and Sobolev inequality, given that

s_{0}>0
.

Finally we consider the terms
\mathcal{W}^{\pm}_{1}(w,u)
. We apply duality and further dyadic decompositions as in (
3.50
) and reduce to controlling

\displaystyle\sum_{N\gtrsim 1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}N_{1},N_{2},N_{3},N_{23}\\
N\gtrsim N_{1}\end{subarray}}N^{s-1}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\overline{\mathbf{P}_{N}g}\cdot\mathbf{P}_{\pm,\textup{HI}}\big[\mathbf{P}_{N_{1}}w\,\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}\partial_{x}\mathbf{P}_{N_{23}}(\overline{\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}u}\mathbf{P}_{N_{3}}u)\big]dxdt,

(3.56)

where
g\in L^{2}_{T,x}
with
\|g\|_{L^{2}_{T,x}}\leq 1
.
For the contribution with
\mathbf{P}_{+,\textup{HI}}
in (
3.56
) coming from
\mathcal{W}^{+}_{1}
, we have two cases: (i)
N\sim N_{1}\sim N_{23}
and (ii)
N\sim N_{23}\gg N_{1}
.
Notice that for the contribution with
\mathbf{P}_{-,\textup{HI}}
, we can only have
N\sim N_{1}\gtrsim N_{23}
.

\bullet

Case (i):
\mathcal{W}_{1}^{\pm}
with
N\sim N_{1}\gtrsim N_{23}
.

This case follows immediately by the same argument as in Case 1 of the proof of Lemma
3.6
giving rise to the bound

\displaystyle\|J^{s_{0}}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}.

(3.57)

Note that here we harmlessly replaced
w
by
\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}u
.

\bullet

Case (ii):
\mathcal{W}_{1}^{+}
with
N\sim N_{23}\gtrsim N_{1}
.

This case follows immediately by the same argument as in Case 2 of the proof of Lemma
3.6
giving rise to the same bound as in (
3.57
).

Combining the different cases, we have shown

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|G(u,w)\|_{L^{2}_{T}H^{s-1}_{x}}\lesssim&\|J^{s_{0}}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}^{3}.\end{split}

(3.58)

which completes the proof. ∎

3.5.
INLS regularity properties: more nonlinear estimates

The main goal of this subsection is to establish the analogue of Lemma
3.7
for INLS (
2.16
), as well as on the difference of solutions.

Lemma 3.10

(Estimates for INLS)
.

Let
0<T\leq 1
,
\frac{1}{4}<s<\frac{3}{4}
,
0\leq T\leq 1
, and
u
be a
H^{\infty}(\mathbb{R})
-solution to (
2.16
) on
[0,T]
. Then it holds that:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}\lesssim&T^{\frac{1}{4}-}(1+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{2})\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}_{T}}\\
&+T^{\frac{1}{4}-}(1+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{2})\Big[\|w\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}_{T}}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{3}\Big],\end{split}

(3.59)

where
(v,w)
are the gauged variables in (
3.13
).
Moreover, there exist
\theta_{1},\theta_{2},\theta_{3}>0
such that for any
M\in\mathbb{N}
sufficiently large, it holds that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}&\lesssim\|u_{0}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}_{T}}+\|w\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}_{T}}+TM\|\mathcal{Q}_{h}\|_{\textup{op}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{3}\\
&+T^{\theta_{1}}M^{\theta_{2}}\Big\{\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{3}+\|J^{\frac{1}{4}+}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}^{3}+\|w\|_{X^{\frac{1}{4}+,\frac{1}{2}+}_{T}}^{3}\Big\}\\
&+M^{-\theta_{3}}\Big\{(1+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{2})(\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}+\|w\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}})+\|v\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}_{T}})\Big\},\end{split}

(3.60)

where
\mathcal{Q}_{h}
is as in (
2.15
).

Proof.

The arguments we use here are the same as those we used for obtaining Lemma
3.7
with one key difference: we no longer have the Hardy space assumption so we need new arguments to deal with terms that relied on this assumption. Thus, in the following, we detail the necessary changes to the proof of Lemma
3.7
.

We first consider (
3.59
).
By the recovery formula (
3.17
), we obtain (
3.26
) with the additional term
\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}w\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}
, which is easily estimated by (
3.6
) to give

\displaystyle\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}u\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\hskip 1.42262pt\text{I}\hskip 1.42262pt+\text{I \hskip-7.96677pt I}+\text{I \hskip-8.25128pt I \hskip-8.25128pt I}+T^{\frac{1}{4}-}\|w\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}_{T}},

where
\hskip 1.42262pt\text{I}\hskip 1.42262pt,\text{I \hskip-7.96677pt I}
, and
I I I
are as in (

). The estimates for

and
I I
in (

) and (

, since we used the Hardy space assumption to establish (

).
By writing

\displaystyle\begin{split}\text{I \hskip-8.25128pt I \hskip-8.25128pt I}\leq&\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[e^{-i\beta F}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}u)]\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}\\
&+\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[e^{-i\beta F}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}u)]\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}}+\|J^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{+,HI}}[e^{-i\beta F}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}w)]\|_{\widetilde{L^{4}_{T,x}}},\end{split}

(3.61)

we can proceed in exactly the same way as in Lemma

to handle the contribution from the first two terms, since they have
u=\mathbf{P}_{+}u
.
Arguing as in (

) with (
3.35
), we have

\displaystyle\text{I \hskip-8.25128pt I \hskip-8.25128pt I}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{4}\sum_{N}\bigg\{\sum_{N_{2}\lesssim N}N^{s-\frac{3}{4}}\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}\mathbf{P}_{-,\textup{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}w)\|_{L^{4}_{T}L^{\infty}_{x}}\bigg\}^{2},

and
we now need a replacement for (
3.34
), which due to (

) amounts to adequately controlling

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{N_{2}}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}w)\|_{L^{4}_{T}L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}_{x}}.

(3.62)

However, simply by Sobolev embedding and (

) we have

\displaystyle\eqref{Jsu3}\lesssim\|w\|_{L^{4}_{T}L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}_{x}}\lesssim\|J^{\frac{1}{4}+}w\|_{L^{4}_{T,x}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{4}-}\|w\|_{X^{\frac{1}{4}+,\frac{1}{2}}_{T}}.

Then, combining the estimates above, for
s<\frac{3}{4}
, we have

\displaystyle\text{I \hskip-8.25128pt I \hskip-8.25128pt I}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{4}-}\|u\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}\big(\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}+\|w\|_{X^{\frac{1}{4}+,\frac{1}{2}}_{T}}\big),

completing the proof of (
3.59
).

We now consider (
3.60
). Fixing
M\in\mathbb{N}
sufficiently large, we split
u=\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}u+\mathbf{P}_{>M}u
.
For the high frequency term
\mathbf{P}_{>M}u
, compared to CCM, we again have the extra term
\mathbf{P}_{\textup{HI}}w
which is controlled using (
3.3
), the contributions corresponding to
\hskip 1.42262pt\text{I}\hskip 1.42262pt,\text{I \hskip-7.96677pt I}
in (
3.26
) are estimated analogously, and it remains to consider the term
I I I
which we decompose as in (
3.61
). For the contribution with
\mathbf{P}_{+,\text{HI}}u
, we bound it exactly as we did in obtaining (
3.44
). For the contribution due to
w
, we have

\displaystyle\|D^{s}\mathbf{P}_{>M}\mathbf{P}_{+}[\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}w)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|D^{s}[\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}w)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}},

(3.63)

because of the signs on the frequencies.
By the fractional Leibniz rule, (
2.8
), Bernstein and Sobolev embedding, we have

(
3.63
)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|D^{s}\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}e^{-i\beta F}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{4}_{x}}\|\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}w)\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{4}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}e^{-i\beta F}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T,x}}\|D^{s}\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}w)\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{s-\frac{3}{4}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{2}\|w\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{4}_{x}}+M^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{2}\|D^{s}w\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{s-\frac{3}{4}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}}^{2}\|w\|_{X^{s,\frac{1}{2}+\delta}_{T}}.

Finally, when we have
\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}u
in (
3.61
), we still have

\displaystyle\|D^{s}\mathbf{P}_{>M}\mathbf{P}_{+}[\mathbf{P}_{\textup{hi}}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}u)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|D^{s}[\mathbf{P}_{\gtrsim M}(e^{-i\beta F})\mathbf{P}_{-,\text{hi}}(e^{i\beta F}\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}u)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}L^{2}_{x}},

(3.64)

Then, we estimate exactly as we did for (
3.63
) using Bernstein’s inequality for the term
\mathbf{P}_{\textup{lo}}u
and we obtain

\displaystyle\eqref{IIIHs12}\lesssim M^{s-\frac{3}{4}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{3}.

This completes the estimate for the high frequency part.

For the low frequencies, we need to refine the argument for (
3.37
), and we use the decomposition of the nonlinearity described in Section
3.4
. Namely, applying
\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}
to both sides of (
3.47
) and recalling the definitions in (

), we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}[u\partial_{x}\mathbf{P}_{+}(|u|^{2})]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}G(u,w)+\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}B(u,u,w).

Then, by the Duhamel formula for (
2.16
), Lemma
3.1
, (
3.3
), and (

), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}u_{0}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+2|\beta|T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}G(u,w)\|_{L^{2}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}+2|\beta|\|\mathcal{I}[\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}B(u,u,w)]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+T\|\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}(u\mathcal{Q}_{h}(|u|^{2}))\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{s}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq\|u_{0}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+CT^{\frac{1}{2}}M^{1+s-s_{0}}\|\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}G(u,w)\|_{L^{2}_{T}H^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}+CT^{\delta}M^{\sigma+s-s_{0}}\|\mathbf{P}_{\leq M}B(u,u,w)\|_{X^{s_{0}-\sigma,-\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}_{T}}

\displaystyle\quad+CTM\|\mathcal{Q}_{h}\|_{\text{op}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{4}+}_{x}}^{3},

(3.65)

for
\mathcal{Q}_{h}
as in (
2.15
).
Now we notice that we have already controlled

in the course of proving Lemma
3.9
and we simply apply (
3.58
) with

. For the term with

, we apply Lemma

with

and we take
\sigma=\frac{1}{4}+>0
. This completes the proof.
∎
